<script type="text/javascript">
    /**
     * 将后端返回的时间格式化为 yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm
     */
    function formatTime(value) {
        if (!value) {
            return "—";
        }
        return String(value).replace("T", " ").substring(0, 16);
    }

    /** 当前正在管理的活动编号，未选中时为 null */
    var currentActivityNum = null;
    /** 活动编号与活动名称、是否可结算的对应关系 */
    var activityMeta = {};
    /** 复核模态框实例 */
    var reviewModal = null;
    /** 当前待复核的签到记录 */
    var reviewTarget = {checkinNum: null, isPass: null};

    /**
     * 渲染活动列表（未开始 / 进行中 / 已结束）
     */
E志愿志愿者服务平台 V1.0                                                      第 225 页
    function loadActivityList(url, holderName, tableId, endActivity) {
        $.ajax({
            url: url,
            type: "GET",
            data: {},
            success: function (result) {
                var activities = (result && result.extend) ? (result.extend[holderName] || []) : [];
                var tabValue = ``;
                $.each(activities, function (index, activity) {
                    activityMeta[activity.activityNum] = {
                        name: activity.activityName,
                        endActivity: endActivity
                    };
                    tabValue += `<tr>
                                    <td>${activity.activityName}</td>
                                    <td>${formatTime(activity.activityBegintime)} ~ ${formatTime(activity.activityEndtime)}</td>
                                    <td>${activity.activityLocation || "—"}</td>
                                    <td>${activity.activityNeedpeople || 0}</td>
                                    <td>${activity.activitySettleTime ? "已结算" : "未结算"}</td>
                                    <td>
                                        <button class="btn btn-outline-primary activity_manage_button" type="button" data-activity-num="${activity.activityNum}">签到与时长管理</button>
                                    </td>
                                 </tr>`;
                });
                if (activities.length === 0) {
                    tabValue = `<tr><td colspan="6" class="text-center text-muted">暂无数据</td></tr>`;
                }
                $("#" + tableId + " tbody").html(tabValue);
            },
            error: function (result) {
                console.log(result);
            }
        });
    }

    /**
     * 选中活动，展示签到码与签到记录管理区
     */
    function selectActivity(activityNum) {
        currentActivityNum = activityNum;
        var meta = activityMeta[activityNum] || {};
        $("#manage_activity_name").text(meta.name ? "：" + meta.name : "");
        if (meta.endActivity) {
            $("#settle_activity").removeClass("d-none");
        } else {
            $("#settle_activity").addClass("d-none");
        }
        $("#manageArea").removeClass("d-none");
        loadCheckinCode();
        loadCheckinReviewList();
E志愿志愿者服务平台 V1.0                                                      第 226 页
        document.getElementById("manageArea").scrollIntoView();
    }

    /**
     * 查询当前活动的现场签到码
     */
    function loadCheckinCode() {
        if (currentActivityNum === null) {
            return;
        }
        $.ajax({
            url: "/checkin/code",
            type: "GET",
            data: {
                "activityNum": currentActivityNum
            },
            success: function (result) {
                var checkinCode = (result && result.extend) ? result.extend.checkinCode : null;
                $("#checkin_code").text(checkinCode ? checkinCode : "尚未生成");
            },
            error: function (result) {
                console.log(result);
            }
        });
    }

    /**
     * 生成 / 重新生成现场签到码
     */
    function generateCheckinCode(refresh) {
        if (currentActivityNum === null) {
            return;
        }
        $.ajax({
            url: "/checkin/code",
            type: "POST",
            data: {
                "activityNum": currentActivityNum,
                "refresh": refresh
            },
            success: function (result) {
                if (result && result.extend && result.extend.checkinCode) {
                    $("#checkin_code").text(result.extend.checkinCode);
                    alert("签到码已生成，请在活动现场向志愿者公布");
                } else {
                    alert((result && result.msg) ? result.msg : "签到码生成失败");
                }
            },
            error: function (result) {
                console.log(result);
E志愿志愿者服务平台 V1.0                                                      第 227 页
            }
        });
    }

    /**
     * 复核状态文案：null 待复核、"1" 已确认、"2" 已驳回
     */
    function checkinStateText(checkinTimecheck) {
        if (checkinTimecheck === "1") {
            return "已确认";
        }
        if (checkinTimecheck === "2") {
            return "已驳回";
        }
        return "待复核";
    }

    /**
     * 轨迹标记文案："1" 正常、"2" 异常（异常标红）
     */
    function checkinFlagText(checkinFlag) {
        if (checkinFlag === "1") {
            return "正常";
        }
        if (checkinFlag === "2") {
            return '<span style="color: red">异常</span>';
        }
        return "—";
    }

    /**
     * 复核操作列：待复核显示确认 / 驳回按钮，已复核显示复核备注
     */
    function reviewControlTd(item) {
        if (!item.checkinTimecheck) {
            return `<td class="text-center">
                        <div class="btn-group btn-group-pill" role="group" aria-label="时长复核">
                            <button class="btn btn-outline-primary" type="button" onclick="openReviewModal(${item.checkinNum},1)">确认时长</button>
                            <button class="btn btn-outline-danger" type="button" onclick="openReviewModal(${item.checkinNum},2)">驳回</button>
                        </div>
                    </td>`;
        }
        return `<td class="text-center">${item.checkinRemark || "—"}</td>`;
    }

    /**
     * 查询当前活动的签到与时长记录
     */
    function loadCheckinReviewList() {
        if (currentActivityNum === null) {
E志愿志愿者服务平台 V1.0                                                      第 228 页
            return;
        }
        $.ajax({
            url: "/checkin/reviewList",
            type: "GET",
            data: {
                "activityNum": currentActivityNum
            },
            success: function (result) {
                var checkins = (result && result.extend) ? (result.extend.checkins || []) : [];
                var tabValue = ``;
                $.each(checkins, function (index, item) {
                    tabValue += `<tr>
                                    <td>${item.volunteerId}</td>
                                    <td>${item.volunteerName}</td>
                                    <td>${item.volunteerTel || "—"}</td>
                                    <td>${formatTime(item.checkinBegintime)}</td>
                                    <td>${formatTime(item.checkinEndtime)}</td>
                                    <td>${(item.checkinDuration === null || item.checkinDuration === undefined) ? "—" : item.checkinDuration + " 小时"}</td>
                                    <td>${(item.checkinDistance === null || item.checkinDistance === undefined) ? "—" : item.checkinDistance}</td>
                                    <td>${checkinFlagText(item.checkinFlag)}</td>
                                    <td>${checkinStateText(item.checkinTimecheck)}</td>
                                    ${reviewControlTd(item)}
                                 </tr>`;
                });
                if (checkins.length === 0) {
                    tabValue = `<tr><td colspan="10" class="text-center text-muted">暂无数据</td></tr>`;
                }
                $("#checkinReview_table tbody").html(tabValue);
            },
            error: function (result) {
                console.log(result);
            }
        });
    }

    /**
     * 打开复核模态框，isPass 为 1 确认时长、2 驳回
     */
    function openReviewModal(checkinNum, isPass) {
        reviewTarget.checkinNum = checkinNum;
        reviewTarget.isPass = isPass;
        $("#review_result").text(isPass === 1 ? "确认时长" : "驳回");
        $("#review_remark").val("");
        if (!reviewModal) {
            reviewModal = new bootstrap.Modal(document.getElementById("reviewModal"));
        }
        reviewModal.show();
    }

E志愿志愿者服务平台 V1.0                                                      第 229 页
    /**
     * 提交时长复核结果
     */
    function submitReview() {
        $.ajax({
            url: "/checkin/review",
            type: "POST",
            data: {
                "checkinNum": reviewTarget.checkinNum,
                "isPass": reviewTarget.isPass,
                "remark": $("#review_remark").val()
            },
            success: function (result) {
                if (result && result.code === 100) {
                    if (reviewModal) {
                        reviewModal.hide();
                    }
                    alert(result.msg || "复核完成");
                    loadCheckinReviewList();
                } else {
                    alert((result && result.msg) ? result.msg : "复核失败");
                }
            },
            error: function (result) {
                console.log(result);
            }
        });
    }

    /**
     * 活动结算，结算后刷新签到记录与活动列表
     */
    function settleActivity() {
        if (currentActivityNum === null) {
            return;
        }
        if (!confirm("确定对该活动进行结算吗？结算后将记录未签到志愿者的爽约情况。")) {
            return;
        }
        $.ajax({
            url: "/org/settle",
            type: "POST",
            data: {
                "activityNum": currentActivityNum
            },
            success: function (result) {
                alert((result && result.msg) ? result.msg : "活动结算完成");
                loadCheckinReviewList();
                loadActivityList("/activity/findNotStartActivityByNumAndState", "auditActivity", "notStartActivity_table", false);
                loadActivityList("/activity/findCarryActivityByNumAndState", "carryActivity", "carryActivity_table", false);
E志愿志愿者服务平台 V1.0                                                      第 230 页
                loadActivityList("/activity/findEndActivityByNumAndState", "endActivity", "endActivity_table", true);
            },
            error: function (result) {
                console.log(result);
            }
        });
    }

    //一进网页就加载三个活动列表
    $(document).ready(function () {
        loadActivityList("/activity/findNotStartActivityByNumAndState", "auditActivity", "notStartActivity_table", false);
        loadActivityList("/activity/findCarryActivityByNumAndState", "carryActivity", "carryActivity_table", false);
        loadActivityList("/activity/findEndActivityByNumAndState", "endActivity", "endActivity_table", true);

        $(document).on("click", ".activity_manage_button", function () {
            selectActivity($(this).attr("data-activity-num"));
        });
        $("#generate_checkin_code").click(function () {
            generateCheckinCode(false);
        });
        $("#refresh_checkin_code").click(function () {
            generateCheckinCode(true);
        });
        $("#settle_activity").click(function () {
            settleActivity();
        });
        $("#review_submit").click(function () {
            submitReview();
        });
    });
</script>
</body>
</html>

<script type="text/javascript">
    /**
     * 将后端返回的时间格式化为 yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm
     */
    function formatTime(value) {
        if (!value) {
            return "—";
        }
        return String(value).replace("T", " ").substring(0, 16);
    }

    /**
     * 查询当前登录组织在指定状态下申报的志愿活动
     */
    function loadActivity(url, holderName, onSuccess) {
        $.ajax({
            url: url,
            type: "GET",
            success: function (result) {
                var list = (result && result.extend) ? result.extend[holderName] : null;
                if (onSuccess) {
                    onSuccess(list || []);
                }
            },
            error: function (result) {
                console.log(result);
            }
        });
    }

    /**
     * 渲染审核中的活动列表
     */
    function renderActivityTable(activities) {
        var $body = $("#activity_table").empty();
        if (activities.length === 0) {
            $body.append('<tr><td colspan="7" class="text-center text-muted">暂无审核中的志愿活动</td></tr>');
            return;
        }
        $.each(activities, function (index, activity) {
            var row = "<tr>"
                + "<td>" + activity.activityName + "</td>"
                + "<td>" + (activity.activityLocation || "—") + "</td>"
                + "<td>" + formatTime(activity.activityBegintime) + "</td>"
                + "<td>" + formatTime(activity.activityEndtime) + "</td>"
E志愿志愿者服务平台 V1.0                                                      第 237 页
                + "<td>" + formatTime(activity.activitySignddl) + "</td>"
                + "<td>" + (activity.activityNeedpeople || 0) + "</td>"
                + '<td><a class="btn btn-primary btn-xs" href="act_audit.html">查看</a></td>'
                + "</tr>";
            $body.append(row);
        });
    }

    /**
     * 查询当前登录组织的资料
     */
    function loadOrganization() {
        $.ajax({
            url: "/org/info",
            type: "GET",
            success: function (result) {
                if (!result || !result.extend) {
                    return;
                }
                var org = result.extend.organization;
                $("#organization_name").text(org.organizationName);
                $("#organization_name_detail").text(org.organizationName);
                $("#organization_id").text(org.organizationId);
                $("#organization_date").text(formatTime(org.organizationEstablishdate).substring(0, 10));
                $("#organization_introduction").text(org.organizationIntroduction || "—");
            },
            error: function (result) {
                console.log(result);
            }
        });
    }

    $(document).ready(function () {
        loadActivity("/activity/findAuditActivityByNumAndState", "auditActivity", function (list) {
            $("#count_audit").text(list.length);
            renderActivityTable(list);
        });
        loadActivity("/activity/findNotStartActivityByNumAndState", "auditActivity", function (list) {
            $("#count_not_start").text(list.length);
        });
        loadActivity("/activity/findCarryActivityByNumAndState", "carryActivity", function (list) {
            $("#count_carry").text(list.length);
        });
        loadActivity("/activity/findEndActivityByNumAndState", "endActivity", function (list) {
            $("#count_end").text(list.length);
        });
        loadOrganization();
    });
</script>
</body>
E志愿志愿者服务平台 V1.0                                                      第 238 页
</html>

E志愿志愿者服务平台 V1.0                                                      第 243 页

    $("#info_change").click(function () {
        var name = $("#org_name").val();
        var introduction = $("#org_introduction").val();
        console.log(name);
        console.log(introduction);
        $.ajax({
            url: "/org/infoChange",
            type: "POST",
            data:{
                "name":name,
                "introduction":introduction
            },
            success: function (result) {
                if (result) {
                    alert("修改成功");
                } else {
                    alert("修改失败");
                }
            },
            error: function (result) {
                console.log(result);
            }
        })
    })
</script>

<script type="text/javascript">

    $("#activity_name").blur(function () {
        var name = $("#activity_name").val();
        $.ajax({
            url: "/org/checkname",
            type: "GET",
            data: {
                "name": name
            },
            success: function (result) {
                if (result) {
                    $("#sp").val("");
                    $("#sp").html("   √   ").css("color", "green");
                } else {
                    $("#sp").val("");
                    $("#sp").html("   活动名称重复   ").css("color", "red");
                }
            }
        })
    });

    $("#upactivity_submit").click(function () {
        var name = $("#activity_name").val();
        var content = $("#activity_content").val();
        var startTime = $("#start_time").val();
        var endTime = $("#end_time").val();
E志愿志愿者服务平台 V1.0                                                      第 251 页
        var location = $("#activity_location").val();
        var affair = $("#activity_affair").val();
        var need = $("#activity_need").val();
        var sign_ddl = $("#sign_ddl").val();
        var tags = collectSkillTags();
        var latitude = $("#activity_latitude").val();
        var longitude = $("#activity_longitude").val();
        var radius = $("#activity_radius").val();

        $.ajax({
            url: "/org/upact",
            type: "POST",
            data: {
                "name": name,
                "content": content,
                "startTime": startTime,
                "endTime": endTime,
                "location": location,
                "affair": affair,
                "need": need,
                "sign_ddl": sign_ddl,
                "tags": tags,
                "latitude": latitude,
                "longitude": longitude,
                "radius": radius
            },
            success: function (result) {
                if (result) {
                    alert(result);
                    window.location.href = "act_audit.html";
                } else {
                }
            },
            error: function (result) {
                console.log(result);
            }
        })
    })

    /**
     * 收集表单中选中的服务技能标签，使用英文逗号连接
     */
    function collectSkillTags() {
        var tags = [];
        $(".skill_checkbox:checked").each(function () {
            tags.push($(this).val());
        });
        return tags.join(",");
    }

E志愿志愿者服务平台 V1.0                                                      第 252 页
    /**
     * 使用浏览器定位填充活动地点的纬度与经度
     */
    $("#locate_activity").click(function () {
        if (!navigator.geolocation) {
            alert("当前浏览器不支持定位功能，请手动填写活动定位");
            return;
        }
        navigator.geolocation.getCurrentPosition(function (position) {
            $("#activity_latitude").val(position.coords.latitude.toFixed(6));
            $("#activity_longitude").val(position.coords.longitude.toFixed(6));
        }, function () {
            alert("定位失败，请检查浏览器定位权限后重试");
        });
    });
</script>

<script type="text/javascript">
    /**
     * 将后端返回的时间格式化为 yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm
     */
    function formatTime(value) {
        if (!value) {
            return "—";
        }
        return String(value).replace("T", " ").substring(0, 16);
    }

    /**
     * 服务时长展示：null 显示 “—”，否则保留一位小数并带上单位
     */
    function formatDuration(value) {
        if (value === null || value === undefined || value === "") {
            return "—";
        }
        return Number(value).toFixed(1) + " 小时";
    }

    //校验失败或接口异常时的红色提示
E志愿志愿者服务平台 V1.0                                                      第 261 页
    function showVerifyError(msg) {
        var box = document.getElementById("verifyResult");
        box.innerHTML = "";
        var tip = document.createElement("p");
        tip.className = "text-danger";
        tip.textContent = msg;
        box.appendChild(tip);
    }

    //校验通过时展示证明卡片
    function showVerifyCard(verification) {
        var box = document.getElementById("verifyResult");
        box.innerHTML = `<div class="card">
                            <div class="card-body">
                                <h4 class="card-title">志愿服务证明校验结果</h4>
                                <ul class="list">
                                    <li>志愿者编号：<span id="verifyVolunteerId"></span></li>
                                    <li>姓名：<span id="verifyVolunteerName"></span></li>
                                    <li>累计服务时长：<span id="verifyTotalDuration"></span></li>
                                    <li>参与志愿活动数：<span id="verifyActivityCount"></span></li>
                                    <li>星级：<span id="verifyStarLevel"></span></li>
                                    <li>签发日期：<span id="verifyIssueDate"></span></li>
                                </ul>
                                <p class="text-success" id="verifyConclusion">校验通过，该证明真实有效</p>
                            </div>
                        </div>`;
        document.getElementById("verifyVolunteerId").textContent = verification.volunteerId || "—";
        document.getElementById("verifyVolunteerName").textContent = verification.volunteerName || "—";
        document.getElementById("verifyTotalDuration").textContent = formatDuration(verification.totalDuration);
        document.getElementById("verifyActivityCount").textContent =
            (verification.activityCount === null || verification.activityCount === undefined) ? "—" : verification.activityCount;
        document.getElementById("verifyStarLevel").textContent = verification.starLevel || "—";
        document.getElementById("verifyIssueDate").textContent = formatTime(verification.issueDate);
    }

    document.getElementById("verifyForm").addEventListener("submit", function (event) {
        event.preventDefault();
        var data = new URLSearchParams();
        data.append("volunteerId", document.getElementById("volunteerId").value.trim());
        data.append("code", document.getElementById("code").value.trim());
        document.getElementById("verifyResult").innerHTML = "";
        fetch("/certificate/verify", {
            method: "POST",
            headers: {"Content-Type": "application/x-www-form-urlencoded;charset=UTF-8"},
            body: data
        }).then(function (response) {
            return response.json();
        }).then(function (result) {
            var verification = result.extend ? result.extend.verification : null;
            if (result.code === 100 && verification && verification.valid === true) {
E志愿志愿者服务平台 V1.0                                                      第 262 页
                showVerifyCard(verification);
            } else {
                showVerifyError(result.msg);
            }
        }).catch(function () {
            showVerifyError("校验服务暂时不可用，请稍后重试");
        });
    });

<script type="text/javascript">
    /**
     * 将后端返回的时间格式化为 yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm
     */
    function formatTime(value) {
        if (!value) {
            return "—";
        }
        return String(value).replace("T", " ").substring(0, 16);
    }

    /**
     * 拼接活动的服务时间区间
     */
E志愿志愿者服务平台 V1.0                                                      第 266 页
    function formatActivityTime(activity) {
        var begin = formatTime(activity.activityBegintime);
        var end = formatTime(activity.activityEndtime);
        if (begin === "—" && end === "—") {
            return "—";
        }
        return begin + " ~ " + end;
    }

    /**
     * 将匹配度转换为 0-100 的整数
     */
    function formatScore(value) {
        var num = Number(value);
        if (isNaN(num) || num < 0) {
            return 0;
        }
        return Math.round(num);
    }

    /**
     * 格式化匹配度（进度条 + 分数）
     */
    function renderScore(value) {
        var score = formatScore(value);
        return '<div class="progress" style="height: 6px; width: 70px; display: inline-block;'
            + ' vertical-align: middle;">'
            + '<div class="progress-bar" role="progressbar" style="width: ' + score + '%;" aria-valuenow="'
            + score + '" aria-valuemin="0" aria-valuemax="100"></div>'
            + '</div> <span class="small">' + score + ' 分</span>';
    }

    /**
     * 格式化活动与志愿者常住定位之间的距离
     */
    function formatDistance(distanceMeters) {
        if (distanceMeters === null || distanceMeters === undefined) {
            return "未设置定位";
        }
        var meters = Number(distanceMeters);
        if (isNaN(meters) || meters < 0) {
            return "未设置定位";
        }
        if (meters < 1000) {
            return Math.round(meters) + " 米";
        }
        return (meters / 1000).toFixed(1) + " 公里";
    }

    /**
E志愿志愿者服务平台 V1.0                                                      第 267 页
     * 格式化活动的技能要求
     */
    function formatSkillNames(skillNames) {
        if (!skillNames) {
            return "—";
        }
        return String(skillNames).replace(/,/g, "、");
    }

    /**
     * 格式化匹配理由
     */
    function formatReasons(reasons) {
        if (!reasons || reasons.length === 0) {
            return "—";
        }
        return reasons.join("；");
    }

    /**
     * 查询当前登录志愿者资料，并刷新侧边栏身份信息
     */
    var volunteerProfile = null;
    var profileRequest = null;

    function loadProfile(onLoaded) {
        profileRequest = $.ajax({
            url: "/volunteer/profile",
            type: "GET",
            success: function (result) {
                if (!result || result.code !== 100 || !result.extend || !result.extend.profile) {
                    if (result && result.msg) {
                        alert(result.msg);
                    }
                    return;
                }
                volunteerProfile = result.extend.profile;
                $("#sidebar_volunteer_name").text(volunteerProfile.volunteerName || "志愿者");
                $("#sidebar_volunteer_id").text("编号：" + (volunteerProfile.volunteerId || "—"));
                if (onLoaded) {
                    onLoaded(volunteerProfile);
                }
            },
            error: function (result) {
                console.log(result);
            }
        });
        return profileRequest;
    }

E志愿志愿者服务平台 V1.0                                                      第 268 页
    /**
     * 确保已获取志愿者档案（报名前校验技能标签与常住定位）
     */
    function ensureProfile(callback) {
        if (volunteerProfile) {
            callback(volunteerProfile);
            return;
        }
        if (!profileRequest) {
            loadProfile(callback);
            return;
        }
        profileRequest.always(function () {
            callback(volunteerProfile);
        });
    }

    /**
     * 渲染智能匹配活动列表
     */
    function renderPlaza(activities) {
        var html = "";
        if (!activities || activities.length === 0) {
            html = '<tr><td colspan="9" class="text-center text-muted">暂无数据</td></tr>';
        } else {
            $.each(activities, function (index, activity) {
                html += "<tr>"
                    + "<td>" + activity.activityName + "</td>"
                    + "<td>" + formatActivityTime(activity) + "</td>"
                    + "<td>" + (activity.activityLocation || "—") + "</td>"
                    + "<td>" + ((activity.activityNeedpeople === null || activity.activityNeedpeople === undefined)
                        ? "—" : activity.activityNeedpeople + " 人") + "</td>"
                    + "<td>" + formatDistance(activity.distanceMeters) + "</td>"
                    + "<td>" + formatSkillNames(activity.activitySkillNames) + "</td>"
                    + "<td>" + renderScore(activity.matchScore) + "</td>"
                    + "<td>" + formatReasons(activity.reasons) + "</td>"
                    + '<td><button class="btn btn-primary btn-xs" type="button" onclick="applyActivity('
                    + activity.activityNum + ')">报名</button></td>'
                    + "</tr>";
            });
        }
        $("#plaza_table").html(html);
    }

    /**
     * 加载智能匹配活动广场数据
     */
    function loadPlaza() {
        $.ajax({
            url: "/volunteer/plaza",
E志愿志愿者服务平台 V1.0                                                      第 269 页
            type: "GET",
            success: function (result) {
                if (!result || result.code !== 100 || !result.extend) {
                    if (result && result.msg) {
                        alert(result.msg);
                    }
                    return;
                }
                renderPlaza(result.extend.activities);
            },
            error: function (result) {
                console.log(result);
            }
        });
    }

    /**
     * 报名活动：报名前必须完善技能标签与常住定位
     */
    function applyActivity(activityNum) {
        ensureProfile(function (profile) {
            var skills = (profile && profile.skills) ? profile.skills : [];
            var hasLocation = !!(profile && profile.latitude !== null && profile.latitude !== undefined
                && profile.longitude !== null && profile.longitude !== undefined);
            if (!profile || skills.length === 0 || !hasLocation) {
                if (confirm("请先在“我的档案”中完善技能标签与常住定位，是否现在前往完善？")) {
                    window.location.href = "profile.html";
                }
                return;
            }
            $.ajax({
                url: "/volunteer/apply",
                type: "POST",
                data: {activityNum: activityNum},
                success: function (result) {
                    alert((result && result.msg) ? result.msg : "操作完成");
                    if (result && result.code === 100) {
                        loadPlaza();
                    }
                },
                error: function (result) {
                    console.log(result);
                    alert("网络异常，请稍后重试");
                }
            });
        });
    }

    $(document).ready(function () {
        loadProfile();
E志愿志愿者服务平台 V1.0                                                      第 270 页
        loadPlaza();
    });
</script>
</body>
</html>

<script type="text/javascript">
    /**
     * 将后端返回的时间格式化为 yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm
     */
    function formatTime(value) {
        if (!value) {
            return "—";
        }
E志愿志愿者服务平台 V1.0                                                      第 275 页
        return String(value).replace("T", " ").substring(0, 16);
    }

    /**
     * 将数值格式化为一位小数，非法数值按 0.0 处理
     */
    function formatHours(value) {
        var num = Number(value);
        if (isNaN(num)) {
            num = 0;
        }
        return num.toFixed(1);
    }

    /**
     * 渲染服务明细列表
     */
    function renderRecords(records) {
        var html = "";
        if (!records || records.length === 0) {
            html = '<tr><td colspan="4" class="text-center text-muted">暂无数据</td></tr>';
        } else {
            $.each(records, function (index, record) {
                html += "<tr>"
                    + "<td>" + (record.activityName || "—") + "</td>"
                    + "<td>" + (record.activityId || "—") + "</td>"
                    + "<td>" + formatTime(record.serviceDate).substring(0, 10) + "</td>"
                    + "<td>" + ((record.duration === null || record.duration === undefined)
                        ? "—" : formatHours(record.duration) + " 小时") + "</td>"
                    + "</tr>";
            });
        }
        $("#certificate_table").html(html);
    }

    /**
     * 渲染星级进度说明
     */
    function renderNextLevel(certificate) {
        var nextLevelName = certificate.nextLevelName;
        var hoursToNextLevel = Number(certificate.hoursToNextLevel);
        if (!nextLevelName) {
            $("#cert_next_level").text("已达成最高星级，感谢您的持续付出");
            return;
        }
        if (isNaN(hoursToNextLevel) || hoursToNextLevel < 0) {
            hoursToNextLevel = 0;
        }
        $("#cert_next_level").text("当前星级：" + (certificate.starLevel || "—")
            + "；距离「" + nextLevelName + "」还需 " + hoursToNextLevel.toFixed(1) + " 小时");
E志愿志愿者服务平台 V1.0                                                      第 276 页
    }

    /**
     * 渲染志愿服务证明
     */
    function renderCertificate(certificate) {
        $("#sidebar_volunteer_name").text(certificate.volunteerName || "志愿者");
        $("#sidebar_volunteer_id").text("编号：" + (certificate.volunteerId || "—"));

        $("#cert_volunteer_name").text(certificate.volunteerName || "—");
        $("#cert_volunteer_id").text(certificate.volunteerId || "—");
        $("#cert_total_duration").text(formatHours(certificate.totalDuration));
        $("#cert_activity_count").text((certificate.activityCount === null
            || certificate.activityCount === undefined) ? "—" : certificate.activityCount);
        $("#cert_star_level").text(certificate.starLevel || "—");
        $("#cert_issue_date").text(certificate.issueDate || "—");
        $("#cert_verify_code").text(certificate.verifyCode || "—");

        renderNextLevel(certificate);
        renderRecords(certificate.records);
    }

    /**
     * 查询当前登录志愿者的志愿服务证明
     */
    function loadCertificate() {
        $.ajax({
            url: "/volunteer/certificate",
            type: "GET",
            success: function (result) {
                if (!result || result.code !== 100 || !result.extend || !result.extend.certificate) {
                    if (result && result.msg) {
                        alert(result.msg);
                    }
                    return;
                }
                renderCertificate(result.extend.certificate);
            },
            error: function (result) {
                console.log(result);
            }
        });
    }

    $(document).ready(function () {
        loadCertificate();
    });
</script>
</body>
</html>

<script type="text/javascript">
    /**
     * 将后端返回的时间格式化为 yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm
     */
    function formatTime(value) {
        if (!value) {
            return "—";
        }
        return String(value).replace("T", " ").substring(0, 16);
    }

    /**
     * 将数值格式化为一位小数，非法数值按 0.0 处理
     */
    function formatHours(value) {
        var num = Number(value);
        if (isNaN(num)) {
            num = 0;
        }
        return num.toFixed(1);
    }

    /**
     * 拼接活动的服务时间区间
     */
    function formatActivityTime(activity) {
        var begin = formatTime(activity.activityBegintime);
        var end = formatTime(activity.activityEndtime);
        if (begin === "—" && end === "—") {
            return "—";
        }
        return begin + " ~ " + end;
    }

    /**
     * 查询当前登录志愿者资料，并刷新侧边栏身份信息
     */
    var volunteerProfile = null;

    function loadProfile(onLoaded) {
        $.ajax({
            url: "/volunteer/profile",
            type: "GET",
            success: function (result) {
                if (!result || result.code !== 100 || !result.extend || !result.extend.profile) {
                    if (result && result.msg) {
E志愿志愿者服务平台 V1.0                                                      第 283 页
                        alert(result.msg);
                    }
                    return;
                }
                volunteerProfile = result.extend.profile;
                $("#sidebar_volunteer_name").text(volunteerProfile.volunteerName || "志愿者");
                $("#sidebar_volunteer_id").text("编号：" + (volunteerProfile.volunteerId || "—"));
                if (onLoaded) {
                    onLoaded(volunteerProfile);
                }
            },
            error: function (result) {
                console.log(result);
            }
        });
    }

    /**
     * 渲染统计卡与成长进度
     */
    function renderProfile(profile) {
        $("#stat_duration").text(formatHours(profile.volunteerTotalDuration));
        $("#stat_credit").text((profile.volunteerCredit === null || profile.volunteerCredit === undefined)
            ? "—" : profile.volunteerCredit);
        $("#stat_star").text(profile.starLevel || "—");

        var nextLevelName = profile.nextLevelName;
        var nextLevelHours = Number(profile.nextLevelHours);
        var hoursToNextLevel = Number(profile.hoursToNextLevel);
        if (!nextLevelName || isNaN(nextLevelHours) || nextLevelHours <= 0) {
            $("#growth_text").text("已达成最高星级，感谢您的持续付出");
            $("#growth_progress").css("width", "100%").attr("aria-valuenow", 100);
            return;
        }
        if (isNaN(hoursToNextLevel) || hoursToNextLevel < 0) {
            hoursToNextLevel = 0;
        }
        var achieved = nextLevelHours - hoursToNextLevel;
        if (achieved < 0) {
            achieved = 0;
        }
        var percent = Math.round(achieved / nextLevelHours * 100);
        if (percent > 100) {
            percent = 100;
        }
        $("#growth_text").text("距离「" + nextLevelName + "」还需 " + hoursToNextLevel.toFixed(1) + " 小时");
        $("#growth_progress").css("width", percent + "%").attr("aria-valuenow", percent);
    }

    /**
E志愿志愿者服务平台 V1.0                                                      第 284 页
     * 渲染“为你推荐”（取匹配度最高的前 3 条活动）
     */
    function renderRecommend(activities) {
        var $holder = $("#recommend_list").empty();
        if (!activities || activities.length === 0) {
            $holder.append('<div class="col-12 text-center text-muted">暂无数据</div>');
            return;
        }
        var html = "";
        $.each(activities.slice(0, 3), function (index, activity) {
            html += '<div class="col-md-4">'
                + '<div class="card o-hidden">'
                + '<div class="card-body">'
                + '<h6 class="mb-1">' + activity.activityName + '</h6>'
                + '<p class="mb-1 text-muted small">服务时间：' + formatActivityTime(activity) + '</p>'
                + '<p class="mb-1 text-muted small">活动地点：' + (activity.activityLocation || "—") + '</p>'
                + '<p class="mb-2">匹配度：<span class="badge badge-primary">' + formatScore(activity.matchScore) + ' 分</span></p>'
                + renderReasons(activity.reasons)
                + '<button class="btn btn-primary btn-sm" type="button" onclick="applyActivity('
                + activity.activityNum + ')">报名</button>'
                + '</div>'
                + '</div>'
                + '</div>';
        });
        $holder.html(html);
    }

    /**
     * 将匹配度转换为 0-100 的整数
     */
    function formatScore(value) {
        var num = Number(value);
        if (isNaN(num) || num < 0) {
            return 0;
        }
        return Math.round(num);
    }

    /**
     * 渲染匹配理由列表
     */
    function renderReasons(reasons) {
        if (!reasons || reasons.length === 0) {
            return '<p class="mb-2 text-muted small">暂无匹配理由</p>';
        }
        var html = '<ul class="list-unstyled mb-2 small">';
        $.each(reasons, function (index, reason) {
            html += '<li>• ' + reason + '</li>';
        });
        html += '</ul>';
E志愿志愿者服务平台 V1.0                                                      第 285 页
        return html;
    }

    /**
     * 渲染“我的报名”列表（仅展示前 5 条）
     */
    function renderParticipations(participations) {
        var html = "";
        if (!participations || participations.length === 0) {
            html = '<tr><td colspan="4" class="text-center text-muted">暂无数据</td></tr>';
        } else {
            $.each(participations.slice(0, 5), function (index, item) {
                html += "<tr>"
                    + "<td>" + item.activityName + "</td>"
                    + "<td>" + formatTime(item.participateApplytime) + "</td>"
                    + '<td><span class="' + applyStateClass(item.participateApplystate) + '">'
                    + (item.participateApplystateText || "—") + '</span></td>'
                    + "<td>" + ((item.participateDuration === null || item.participateDuration === undefined)
                        ? "—" : formatHours(item.participateDuration) + " 小时") + "</td>"
                    + "</tr>";
            });
        }
        $("#participation_table").html(html);
    }

    /**
     * 报名状态对应的文字颜色
     */
    function applyStateClass(state) {
        if (state === "1") {
            return "text-success";
        }
        if (state === "2") {
            return "text-danger";
        }
        if (state === "3") {
            return "text-warning";
        }
        return "text-muted";
    }

    /**
     * 加载智能匹配活动广场数据
     */
    function loadPlaza() {
        $.ajax({
            url: "/volunteer/plaza",
            type: "GET",
            success: function (result) {
                if (!result || result.code !== 100 || !result.extend) {
E志愿志愿者服务平台 V1.0                                                      第 286 页
                    if (result && result.msg) {
                        alert(result.msg);
                    }
                    return;
                }
                renderRecommend(result.extend.activities);
            },
            error: function (result) {
                console.log(result);
            }
        });
    }

    /**
     * 加载我的报名列表
     */
    function loadParticipations() {
        $.ajax({
            url: "/volunteer/myParticipations",
            type: "GET",
            success: function (result) {
                if (!result || result.code !== 100 || !result.extend) {
                    if (result && result.msg) {
                        alert(result.msg);
                    }
                    return;
                }
                var participations = result.extend.participations || [];
                $("#stat_activity").text(participations.length);
                renderParticipations(participations);
            },
            error: function (result) {
                console.log(result);
            }
        });
    }

    /**
     * 报名推荐活动，返回提示直接展示给志愿者
     */
    function applyActivity(activityNum) {
        $.ajax({
            url: "/volunteer/apply",
            type: "POST",
            data: {activityNum: activityNum},
            success: function (result) {
                alert((result && result.msg) ? result.msg : "操作完成");
                if (result && result.code === 100) {
                    loadPlaza();
                    loadParticipations();
E志愿志愿者服务平台 V1.0                                                      第 287 页
                }
            },
            error: function (result) {
                console.log(result);
                alert("网络异常，请稍后重试");
            }
        });
    }

    $(document).ready(function () {
        loadProfile(function (profile) {
            renderProfile(profile);
        });
        loadPlaza();
        loadParticipations();
    });
</script>
</body>
</html>

    /**
     * 将后端返回的时间格式化为 yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm
     */
    function formatTime(value) {
        if (!value) {
            return "—";
        }
        return String(value).replace("T", " ").substring(0, 16);
    }

    /**
     * 将数值格式化为一位小数，非法数值按 0.0 处理
     */
    function formatHours(value) {
        var num = Number(value);
        if (isNaN(num)) {
            num = 0;
E志愿志愿者服务平台 V1.0                                                      第 292 页
        }
        return num.toFixed(1);
    }

    /**
     * 拼接活动的服务时间区间
     */
    function formatActivityTime(item) {
        var begin = formatTime(item.activityBegintime);
        var end = formatTime(item.activityEndtime);
        if (begin === "—" && end === "—") {
            return "—";
        }
        return begin + " ~ " + end;
    }

    /**
     * 查询当前登录志愿者资料，并刷新侧边栏身份信息
     */
    function loadProfile() {
        $.ajax({
            url: "/volunteer/profile",
            type: "GET",
            success: function (result) {
                if (!result || result.code !== 100 || !result.extend || !result.extend.profile) {
                    if (result && result.msg) {
                        alert(result.msg);
                    }
                    return;
                }
                var profile = result.extend.profile;
                $("#sidebar_volunteer_name").text(profile.volunteerName || "志愿者");
                $("#sidebar_volunteer_id").text("编号：" + (profile.volunteerId || "—"));
            },
            error: function (result) {
                console.log(result);
            }
        });
    }

    /**
     * 报名状态对应的文字颜色
     */
    function applyStateHtml(item) {
        var stateClass = "text-muted";
        if (item.participateApplystate === "1") {
            stateClass = "text-success";
        } else if (item.participateApplystate === "2") {
            stateClass = "text-danger";
        } else if (item.participateApplystate === "3") {
E志愿志愿者服务平台 V1.0                                                      第 293 页
            stateClass = "text-warning";
        }
        return '<span class="' + stateClass + '">' + (item.participateApplystateText || "—") + '</span>';
    }

    /**
     * 服务时长复核状态（null 待复核、1 已确认、2 已驳回）
     */
    function timecheckHtml(value) {
        if (value !== null && value !== undefined && value !== "" && String(value) === "1") {
            return '<span class="badge badge-success">已确认</span>';
        }
        if (value !== null && value !== undefined && value !== "" && String(value) === "2") {
            return '<span class="badge badge-danger">已驳回</span>';
        }
        return '<span class="badge badge-secondary">待复核</span>';
    }

    /**
     * 操作列按钮：签到、签退、撤回报名（签到与签退互斥）
     */
    function actionHtml(item) {
        var buttons = "";
        if (item.canCheckin) {
            buttons += '<button class="btn btn-primary btn-xs" type="button" onclick="prepareCheckin('
                + item.activityNum + ')" data-bs-toggle="modal" data-bs-target="#checkinModal">签到</button> ';
        }
        if (item.canCheckout) {
            buttons += '<button class="btn btn-info btn-xs" type="button" onclick="checkoutActivity('
                + item.activityNum + ')">签退</button> ';
        }
        if (item.canCancel) {
            buttons += '<button class="btn btn-outline-danger btn-xs" type="button" onclick="cancelParticipation('
                + item.participateNum + ')">撤回报名</button>';
        }
        return buttons ? buttons : "—";
    }

    /**
     * 渲染我的报名列表
     */
    function renderParticipations(participations) {
        var html = "";
        if (!participations || participations.length === 0) {
            html = '<tr><td colspan="8" class="text-center text-muted">暂无数据</td></tr>';
        } else {
            $.each(participations, function (index, item) {
                html += "<tr>"
                    + "<td>" + item.activityName + "</td>"
                    + "<td>" + formatActivityTime(item) + "</td>"
E志愿志愿者服务平台 V1.0                                                      第 294 页
                    + "<td>" + (item.activityLocation || "—") + "</td>"
                    + "<td>" + formatTime(item.participateApplytime) + "</td>"
                    + "<td>" + applyStateHtml(item) + "</td>"
                    + "<td>" + ((item.participateDuration === null || item.participateDuration === undefined)
                        ? "—" : formatHours(item.participateDuration) + " 小时") + "</td>"
                    + "<td>" + timecheckHtml(item.participateTimecheck) + "</td>"
                    + "<td>" + actionHtml(item) + "</td>"
                    + "</tr>";
            });
        }
        $("#participation_table").html(html);
    }

    /**
     * 加载我的报名列表
     */
    function loadParticipations() {
        $.ajax({
            url: "/volunteer/myParticipations",
            type: "GET",
            success: function (result) {
                if (!result || result.code !== 100 || !result.extend) {
                    if (result && result.msg) {
                        alert(result.msg);
                    }
                    return;
                }
                renderParticipations(result.extend.participations);
            },
            error: function (result) {
                console.log(result);
            }
        });
    }

    /**
     * 获取浏览器当前定位，取不到时返回空经纬度
     */
    function getPosition(callback) {
        if (!navigator.geolocation) {
            callback("", "");
            return;
        }
        navigator.geolocation.getCurrentPosition(function (position) {
            callback(position.coords.latitude, position.coords.longitude);
        }, function () {
            callback("", "");
        }, {enableHighAccuracy: true, timeout: 10000, maximumAge: 0});
    }

E志愿志愿者服务平台 V1.0                                                      第 295 页
    /**
     * 打开签到弹窗前的准备：记录活动编号并清空上次填写内容
     */
    function prepareCheckin(activityNum) {
        $("#checkin_activity_num").val(activityNum);
        $("#checkin_code").val("");
        $("#checkin_latitude").val("");
        $("#checkin_longitude").val("");
    }

    /**
     * 将浏览器定位结果填充到签到弹窗的经纬度输入框
     */
    function fillCheckinLocation() {
        getPosition(function (latitude, longitude) {
            if (latitude === "" || longitude === "") {
                alert("未能获取当前位置，可直接提交签到，系统将标记为异常并交志愿者组织复核");
                return;
            }
            $("#checkin_latitude").val(latitude);
            $("#checkin_longitude").val(longitude);
        });
    }

    /**
     * 关闭签到弹窗
     */
    function hideCheckinModal() {
        var modalElement = document.getElementById("checkinModal");
        if (window.bootstrap && bootstrap.Modal && modalElement) {
            var modalInstance = bootstrap.Modal.getInstance(modalElement);
            if (modalInstance) {
                modalInstance.hide();
            }
        }
    }

    /**
     * 提交签到（活动编号 + 现场签到码 + 定位）
     */
    function submitCheckin() {
        var code = $.trim($("#checkin_code").val());
        if (!code) {
            alert("请输入现场公布的签到码");
            return;
        }
        $.ajax({
            url: "/checkin/in",
            type: "POST",
            data: {
E志愿志愿者服务平台 V1.0                                                      第 296 页
                activityNum: $("#checkin_activity_num").val(),
                code: code,
                latitude: $("#checkin_latitude").val(),
                longitude: $("#checkin_longitude").val()
            },
            success: function (result) {
                hideCheckinModal();
                alert((result && result.msg) ? result.msg : "操作完成");
                loadParticipations();
            },
            error: function (result) {
                console.log(result);
                hideCheckinModal();
                alert("网络异常，请稍后重试");
                loadParticipations();
            }
        });
    }

    /**
     * 签退：先获取定位，再提交签退
     */
    function checkoutActivity(activityNum) {
        getPosition(function (latitude, longitude) {
            $.ajax({
                url: "/checkin/out",
                type: "POST",
                data: {activityNum: activityNum, latitude: latitude, longitude: longitude},
                success: function (result) {
                    alert((result && result.msg) ? result.msg : "操作完成");
                    loadParticipations();
                },
                error: function (result) {
                    console.log(result);
                    alert("网络异常，请稍后重试");
                    loadParticipations();
                }
            });
        });
    }

    /**
     * 撤回报名
     */
    function cancelParticipation(participateNum) {
        if (!confirm("确认撤回该志愿活动的报名？")) {
            return;
        }
        $.ajax({
            url: "/volunteer/cancel",
E志愿志愿者服务平台 V1.0                                                      第 297 页
            type: "POST",
            data: {participateNum: participateNum},
            success: function (result) {
                alert((result && result.msg) ? result.msg : "操作完成");
                loadParticipations();
            },
            error: function (result) {
                console.log(result);
                alert("网络异常，请稍后重试");
                loadParticipations();
            }
        });
    }

    $(document).ready(function () {
        loadProfile();
        loadParticipations();
    });
</script>
</body>
</html>

    /**
     * 将后端返回的时间格式化为 yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm
     */
    function formatTime(value) {
        if (!value) {
            return "—";
        }
        return String(value).replace("T", " ").substring(0, 16);
    }

    /**
E志愿志愿者服务平台 V1.0                                                      第 304 页
     * 将数值格式化为一位小数，非法数值按 0.0 处理
     */
    function formatHours(value) {
        var num = Number(value);
        if (isNaN(num)) {
            num = 0;
        }
        return num.toFixed(1);
    }

    /**
     * 判断坐标是否已设置
     */
    function hasLocation(latitude, longitude) {
        return latitude !== null && latitude !== undefined && latitude !== ""
            && longitude !== null && longitude !== undefined && longitude !== "";
    }

    /**
     * 渲染志愿者档案（基本信息、服务数据、技能标签、常住定位）
     */
    function renderProfile(profile) {
        $("#sidebar_volunteer_name").text(profile.volunteerName || "志愿者");
        $("#sidebar_volunteer_id").text("编号：" + (profile.volunteerId || "—"));

        $("#profile_name").text(profile.volunteerName || "—");
        $("#profile_id").text(profile.volunteerId || "—");
        $("#profile_tel").text(profile.volunteerTel || "—");
        $("#profile_gender").text(profile.volunteerGender || "—");
        $("#profile_birth").text(formatTime(profile.volunteerBirth).substring(0, 10));

        $("#profile_credit").text((profile.volunteerCredit === null || profile.volunteerCredit === undefined)
            ? "—" : profile.volunteerCredit);
        $("#profile_noshow").text((profile.volunteerNoshow === null || profile.volunteerNoshow === undefined)
            ? "—" : profile.volunteerNoshow);
        $("#profile_duration").text(formatHours(profile.volunteerTotalDuration));
        $("#profile_star").text(profile.starLevel || "—");

        var skills = profile.skills || [];
        $(".skill-checkbox").each(function () {
            $(this).prop("checked", $.inArray($(this).val(), skills) >= 0);
        });

        if (hasLocation(profile.latitude, profile.longitude)) {
            $("#profile_latitude").val(profile.latitude);
            $("#profile_longitude").val(profile.longitude);
            $("#saved_location").text("纬度 " + profile.latitude + "，经度 " + profile.longitude);
        } else {
            $("#profile_latitude").val("");
            $("#profile_longitude").val("");
E志愿志愿者服务平台 V1.0                                                      第 305 页
            $("#saved_location").text("未设置");
        }
    }

    /**
     * 查询当前登录志愿者档案
     */
    function loadProfile() {
        $.ajax({
            url: "/volunteer/profile",
            type: "GET",
            success: function (result) {
                if (!result || result.code !== 100 || !result.extend || !result.extend.profile) {
                    if (result && result.msg) {
                        alert(result.msg);
                    }
                    return;
                }
                renderProfile(result.extend.profile);
            },
            error: function (result) {
                console.log(result);
            }
        });
    }

    /**
     * 获取浏览器当前定位并写回经纬度输入框
     */
    function fillLocation() {
        if (!navigator.geolocation) {
            alert("当前浏览器不支持定位，请手动填写常住坐标");
            return;
        }
        navigator.geolocation.getCurrentPosition(function (position) {
            $("#profile_latitude").val(position.coords.latitude);
            $("#profile_longitude").val(position.coords.longitude);
        }, function () {
            alert("未能获取当前位置，请检查浏览器定位权限后重试");
        }, {enableHighAccuracy: true, timeout: 10000, maximumAge: 0});
    }

    /**
     * 保存档案：技能标签用英文逗号连接，经纬度可为空
     */
    function saveProfile() {
        var skills = $(".skill-checkbox:checked").map(function () {
            return $(this).val();
        }).get().join(",");
        $.ajax({
E志愿志愿者服务平台 V1.0                                                      第 306 页
            url: "/volunteer/profile/save",
            type: "POST",
            data: {
                skills: skills,
                latitude: $.trim($("#profile_latitude").val()),
                longitude: $.trim($("#profile_longitude").val())
            },
            success: function (result) {
                alert((result && result.msg) ? result.msg : "操作完成");
                if (result && result.code === 100) {
                    loadProfile();
                }
            },
            error: function (result) {
                console.log(result);
                alert("网络异常，请稍后重试");
            }
        });
    }

    $(document).ready(function () {
        loadProfile();
    });

package com.evolunteer.entity;

import org.junit.jupiter.api.DisplayName;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

import java.util.List;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertSame;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertTrue;

/**
 * E志愿志愿者服务平台 V1.0
 * <p>
 * 统一响应结果类单元测试：校验成功/失败状态码、提示信息与业务数据装载。
 */
class ApiResponseTest {

    @Test
    @DisplayName("成功结果的状态码与提示信息正确")
    void successShouldCarrySuccessCode() {
        ApiResponse response = ApiResponse.success();

        assertEquals(100, response.getCode());
        assertEquals("处理成功！", response.getMsg());
        assertTrue(response.getExtend().isEmpty());
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("失败结果的状态码与提示信息正确")
    void failShouldCarryFailCode() {
        ApiResponse response = ApiResponse.fail();

        assertEquals(200, response.getCode());
        assertEquals("处理失败！", response.getMsg());
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("添加业务数据后可被读取且支持连续追加")
    void addShouldStoreBusinessData() {
        List<String> activities = List.of("社区爱心助学志愿活动", "敬老院陪伴志愿服务");

        ApiResponse response = ApiResponse.success().add("auditActivity", activities).add("total", activities.size());

        assertSame(activities, response.getExtend().get("auditActivity"));
        assertEquals(2, response.getExtend().get("total"));
    }
E志愿志愿者服务平台 V1.0                                                      第 308 页

源码文件 115：src/test/java/com/evolunteer/enums/VolunteerStarEnumTest.java（单元测试）

package com.evolunteer.enums;

import org.junit.jupiter.api.DisplayName;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertNull;

/**
 * E志愿志愿者服务平台 V1.0
 * <p>
 * 志愿者星级枚举单元测试：校验星级认定阈值、成长进度与最高星级的边界。
 */
class VolunteerStarEnumTest {

    @Test
    @DisplayName("按累计服务时长认定星级，边界值归属上一档")
    void ofShouldMatchStarThresholds() {
        assertEquals("未定级", VolunteerStarEnum.of(0D).getStarName());
        assertEquals("未定级", VolunteerStarEnum.of(99.9).getStarName());
        assertEquals("一星志愿者", VolunteerStarEnum.of(100D).getStarName());
        assertEquals("二星志愿者", VolunteerStarEnum.of(300D).getStarName());
        assertEquals("三星志愿者", VolunteerStarEnum.of(600D).getStarName());
        assertEquals("四星志愿者", VolunteerStarEnum.of(1000D).getStarName());
        assertEquals("五星志愿者", VolunteerStarEnum.of(1500D).getStarName());
        assertEquals("五星志愿者", VolunteerStarEnum.of(9999D).getStarName());
        assertEquals("未定级", VolunteerStarEnum.of(null).getStarName());
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("计算距离下一星级还需的累计服务时长")
    void hoursToNextStarShouldReturnRemainingHours() {
        assertEquals(100D, VolunteerStarEnum.hoursToNextStar(0D));
        assertEquals(97D, VolunteerStarEnum.hoursToNextStar(3D), 0.001);
        assertEquals(0D, VolunteerStarEnum.hoursToNextStar(1500D));
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("下一星级在已达最高星级时为空")
    void nextStarShouldBeNullForTopStar() {
        assertEquals(VolunteerStarEnum.ONE_STAR, VolunteerStarEnum.nextStar(99D));
        assertEquals(VolunteerStarEnum.FIVE_STAR, VolunteerStarEnum.nextStar(1200D));
        assertNull(VolunteerStarEnum.nextStar(1500D));
    }
}

源码文件 116：src/test/java/com/evolunteer/utils/CertificateCodeUtilTest.java（单元测试）

package com.evolunteer.utils;

import org.junit.jupiter.api.DisplayName;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertNotEquals;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertTrue;

/**
 * E志愿志愿者服务平台 V1.0
 * <p>
 * 志愿服务证明校验码工具类单元测试：校验码长度、稳定性与对数据变动的敏感性。
 */
class CertificateCodeUtilTest {

    /**
     * 测试使用的平台密钥
     */
    private static final String SECRET = "evolunteer-test-secret";

    @Test
    @DisplayName("校验码固定为 16 位大写十六进制字符串且可重复计算")
    void codeShouldBeStableHexString() {
        String code = CertificateCodeUtil.code(SECRET, "vol_00001", 3.0, 1);

        assertEquals(16, code.length());
        assertTrue(code.matches("[0-9A-F]{16}"));
        assertEquals(code, CertificateCodeUtil.code(SECRET, "vol_00001", 3.0, 1));
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("累计时长或服务记录数变化时校验码随之变化")
    void codeShouldChangeWithServiceData() {
        String code = CertificateCodeUtil.code(SECRET, "vol_00001", 3.0, 1);

        assertNotEquals(code, CertificateCodeUtil.code(SECRET, "vol_00001", 3.5, 1));
        assertNotEquals(code, CertificateCodeUtil.code(SECRET, "vol_00001", 3.0, 2));
        assertNotEquals(code, CertificateCodeUtil.code(SECRET, "vol_00002", 3.0, 1));
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("密钥不同时同一份服务数据的校验码不同，第三方无法伪造")
    void codeShouldDependOnSecret() {
        assertNotEquals(CertificateCodeUtil.code(SECRET, "vol_00001", 3.0, 1),
                CertificateCodeUtil.code("another-secret", "vol_00001", 3.0, 1));
E志愿志愿者服务平台 V1.0                                                      第 310 页
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("累计时长为空时按 0 小时计算且不抛出异常")
    void codeShouldTolerateNullDuration() {
        String code = CertificateCodeUtil.code(SECRET, "vol_00001", null, null);

        assertEquals(16, code.length());
        assertEquals(code, CertificateCodeUtil.code(SECRET, "vol_00001", 0D, 0));
    }
}

源码文件 117：src/test/java/com/evolunteer/utils/DateTimeParserTest.java（单元测试）

package com.evolunteer.utils;

import org.junit.jupiter.api.DisplayName;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

import java.text.SimpleDateFormat;
import java.util.Date;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertNull;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertThrows;

/**
 * E志愿志愿者服务平台 V1.0
 * <p>
 * 活动时间解析工具类单元测试：校验多种页面时间格式的解析结果与非法输入的提示。
 */
class DateTimeParserTest {

    @Test
    @DisplayName("支持浏览器日期时间控件与历史日期格式")
    void parseShouldSupportMultiplePatterns() throws Exception {
        assertEquals("2026-10-18 09:00:00", format(DateTimeParser.parse("2026-10-18T09:00")));
        assertEquals("2026-10-18 09:00:00", format(DateTimeParser.parse("2026-10-18 09:00")));
        assertEquals("2026-10-18 09:00:00", format(DateTimeParser.parse("2026-10-18T09:00:00")));
        assertEquals("2026-10-18 00:00:00", format(DateTimeParser.parse("2026-10-18")));
        assertEquals("2026-10-18 00:00:00", format(DateTimeParser.parse("10/18/2026")));
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("空白时间文本解析为空值，由存储过程校验必填")
    void parseShouldReturnNullForBlankText() {
        assertNull(DateTimeParser.parse(null));
        assertNull(DateTimeParser.parse(""));
        assertNull(DateTimeParser.parse("   "));
E志愿志愿者服务平台 V1.0                                                      第 311 页
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("非法时间文本抛出可展示给用户的异常")
    void parseShouldRejectInvalidText() {
        IllegalArgumentException exception = assertThrows(IllegalArgumentException.class,
                () -> DateTimeParser.parse("2026年10月18日"));

        assertEquals(true, exception.getMessage().contains("时间格式不正确"));
        assertThrows(IllegalArgumentException.class, () -> DateTimeParser.parse("2026-13-40"));
    }

    /**
     * 将解析出的时间按 yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss 格式化，便于断言
     */
    private String format(Date date) {
        return new SimpleDateFormat("yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss").format(date);
    }
}

package com.evolunteer.utils;

import org.junit.jupiter.api.DisplayName;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertNotEquals;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertThrows;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertTrue;

/**
 * E志愿志愿者服务平台 V1.0
 * <p>
 * 密码摘要工具类单元测试：校验摘要结果、固定长度与空值处理。
 */
class MD5UtilTest {

    @Test
    @DisplayName("相同密码的摘要结果一致且为 32 位十六进制字符串")
    void encodeShouldReturnStableHexCode() {
        String encoded = MD5Util.encode("123456");

        assertEquals("e10adc3949ba59abbe56e057f20f883e", encoded);
        assertEquals(32, encoded.length());
        assertTrue(encoded.matches("[0-9a-f]{32}"));
        assertEquals(encoded, MD5Util.encode("123456"));
    }
E志愿志愿者服务平台 V1.0                                                      第 312 页

    @Test
    @DisplayName("不同密码的摘要结果不同")
    void encodeShouldDifferForDifferentPasswords() {
        assertNotEquals(MD5Util.encode("123456"), MD5Util.encode("1234567"));
        assertNotEquals(MD5Util.encode("abc"), MD5Util.encode("中国志愿者"));
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("中文密码的摘要结果与字符集无关且长度固定")
    void encodeShouldSupportChinesePassword() {
        String encoded = MD5Util.encode("志愿者服务平台");

        assertEquals(32, encoded.length());
        assertEquals(encoded, MD5Util.encode("志愿者服务平台"));
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("空明文在计算摘要前由调用方校验")
    void encodeShouldRejectNullPassword() {
        assertThrows(NullPointerException.class, () -> MD5Util.encode(null));
    }
}

package com.evolunteer.utils;

import org.junit.jupiter.api.DisplayName;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

import java.util.Arrays;
import java.util.Collections;
import java.util.List;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertTrue;

/**
 * E志愿志愿者服务平台 V1.0
 * <p>
 * 供需匹配计算工具类单元测试：校验技能、距离、时间与历史参与四个维度的得分与匹配理由。
 */
class MatchCalculatorTest {

    /**
     * 活动要求的技能标签
     */
    private static final List<String> ACTIVITY_SKILLS = Arrays.asList("社区服务", "文化宣传");
E志愿志愿者服务平台 V1.0                                                      第 313 页

    @Test
    @DisplayName("技能全部命中且距离在 1 公里内、时间不冲突、有历史参与时匹配度为满分")
    void calculateShouldReturnFullScoreForPerfectMatch() {
        MatchCalculator.MatchResult result = MatchCalculator.calculate(
                Arrays.asList("社区服务", "文化宣传"), ACTIVITY_SKILLS, 500,
                Collections.emptyList(), 2);

        assertEquals(100, result.getScore());
        assertEquals(Arrays.asList("社区服务", "文化宣传"), result.getMatchedSkills());
        assertTrue(result.getReasons().contains("技能匹配：社区服务、文化宣传（命中 2/2 项）"));
        assertTrue(result.getReasons().contains("距您 500 米"));
        assertTrue(result.getReasons().contains("与您已报名的活动时间不冲突"));
        assertTrue(result.getReasons().contains("您已参与该组织 2 次活动"));
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("技能部分命中时按命中比例折算技能维度得分")
    void calculateShouldScoreSkillByHitRatio() {
        MatchCalculator.MatchResult result = MatchCalculator.calculate(
                Collections.singletonList("社区服务"), ACTIVITY_SKILLS, 1500,
                Collections.emptyList(), 0);

        // 技能 40 * 1/2 = 20，距离 30 * (20000-1500)/19000 ≈ 29，时间 20，历史 0
        assertEquals(69, result.getScore());
        assertEquals(Collections.singletonList("社区服务"), result.getMatchedSkills());
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("缺少定位时距离维度按中性分值计算并说明原因")
    void calculateShouldUseNeutralScoreWhenDistanceUnknown() {
        MatchCalculator.MatchResult result = MatchCalculator.calculate(
                Collections.emptyList(), Collections.emptyList(), null, Collections.emptyList(), 0);

        // 活动未设置技能要求 20，距离中性 15，时间不冲突 20
        assertEquals(55, result.getScore());
        assertTrue(result.getReasons().contains("未设置常住定位，按中性距离分值计算"));
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("时间冲突时时间维度不得分，并给出冲突活动名称")
    void calculateShouldPenalizeTimeConflict() {
        MatchCalculator.MatchResult result = MatchCalculator.calculate(
                ACTIVITY_SKILLS, ACTIVITY_SKILLS, 300, Collections.singletonList("社区义诊志愿服务"), 1);

        assertEquals(80, result.getScore());
        assertTrue(result.getReasons().contains("与您已报名的「社区义诊志愿服务」时间冲突"));
    }

    @Test
E志愿志愿者服务平台 V1.0                                                      第 314 页
    @DisplayName("距离超过 2 万米时距离维度不得分且提示距离较远")
    void calculateShouldGiveZeroDistanceScoreWhenTooFar() {
        MatchCalculator.MatchResult result = MatchCalculator.calculate(
                Collections.emptyList(), ACTIVITY_SKILLS, 35000, Collections.emptyList(), 0);

        assertEquals(20, result.getScore());
        assertTrue(result.getReasons().contains("距离较远（约 35.0 公里）"));
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("技能标签为空或未登记技能时提示活动要求技能")
    void calculateShouldExplainMissingSkillOverlap() {
        MatchCalculator.MatchResult result = MatchCalculator.calculate(
                Collections.singletonList("信息技术"), ACTIVITY_SKILLS, 2000, Collections.emptyList(), 0);

        assertTrue(result.getMatchedSkills().isEmpty());
        assertTrue(result.getReasons().contains("活动要求技能：社区服务、文化宣传，与您的技能标签暂未重叠"));
    }
}

源码文件 120：src/test/java/com/evolunteer/utils/SecurePasswordEncoderTest.java（单元测试）

package com.evolunteer.utils;

import org.junit.jupiter.api.DisplayName;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertFalse;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertNotEquals;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertTrue;

/**
 * E志愿志愿者服务平台 V1.0
 * <p>
 * 平台密码编码器单元测试：校验加盐摘要、相同密码密文不同，以及历史 MD5 密文的兼容校验与升级判定。
 */
class SecurePasswordEncoderTest {

    private final SecurePasswordEncoder passwordEncoder = new SecurePasswordEncoder();

    @Test
    @DisplayName("加盐摘要可以校验原始密码且密文格式带有算法与迭代次数")
    void encodeShouldProduceVerifiableCipher() {
        String encoded = passwordEncoder.encode("123456");

        assertTrue(encoded.startsWith("pbkdf2$120000$"));
        assertTrue(passwordEncoder.matches("123456", encoded));
        assertFalse(passwordEncoder.matches("1234567", encoded));
E志愿志愿者服务平台 V1.0                                                      第 315 页
        assertFalse(passwordEncoder.matches("123456", encoded + "x"));
        assertFalse(passwordEncoder.matches(null, encoded));
        assertFalse(passwordEncoder.matches("123456", null));
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("同一密码两次加密得到不同密文，避免撞库时批量识破相同密码")
    void encodeShouldUseRandomSalt() {
        String first = passwordEncoder.encode("123456");
        String second = passwordEncoder.encode("123456");

        assertNotEquals(first, second);
        assertTrue(passwordEncoder.matches("123456", first));
        assertTrue(passwordEncoder.matches("123456", second));
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("历史 MD5 密文可以校验通过并需要进行升级")
    void matchesShouldSupportLegacyMd5Cipher() {
        String legacy = MD5Util.encode("123456");

        assertTrue(passwordEncoder.matches("123456", legacy));
        assertFalse(passwordEncoder.matches("654321", legacy));
        assertTrue(passwordEncoder.upgradeEncoding(legacy));
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("加盐密文无需再次升级")
    void upgradeEncodingShouldSkipSaltedCipher() {
        assertFalse(passwordEncoder.upgradeEncoding(passwordEncoder.encode("123456")));
        assertFalse(passwordEncoder.upgradeEncoding(null));
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("非法密文校验返回 false 而不抛出异常")
    void matchesShouldRejectMalformedCipher() {
        assertFalse(passwordEncoder.matches("123456", "pbkdf2$abc$notbase64$notbase64"));
        assertFalse(passwordEncoder.matches("123456", "unknown$format"));
        assertEquals("123456", "123456");
    }
}
